# Campaign API

The Campaign API allows you to create and manage campaigns and insertion orders (IOs). 

This page covers the common endpoints and methods associated with the Campaign API.

  * **Campaigns** are targeted ad runs that operate for a set period of time under specified optimization strategies. 
  * **Insertion orders** are the parent hierarchy to campaigns and they define the contracts between an Advertiser and a media partner, as well as the parameters of an ad campaign.

### More resources
  * [Create a campaign](/quickstart-guides/create-a-campaign-quickstart) quickstart guide
  * [Create a PG campaign](/tutorials/create-a-pg-campaign/) tutorial
  * [Media planning and campaign setup<ExternalLink />](https://help.iqm.com/en/collections/1962110-create-a-campaign) Help Center article

### Authentication

Use the following header parameters for all requests:

  <div class="smallTable">

| Headers  |  |
| ----  | --- |
| `Authentication` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> <span class="required-text">required</span> | Authentication bearer token. <br />See [Sign up and authenticate](/quickstart-guides/authentication-quickstart-guide). |
| `X-IAA-OW-ID` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> <span class="required-text">required</span> | Organization Workspace ID header. |

</div>

### Get campaign details by ID

<CopyUrl method="GET" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v2/cmp/campaign/{campaignId}" operationId="getCampaign" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Get a campaign's basic details and targeting details by ID.

| Path parameters |  |
| ---- | --- |
| <span class="code-text">campaignId</span> <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | [Campaign ID](#get-list-of-campaigns). |


| Query parameters |  |
| ---- | --- |
| <span class="code-text">isSpentRequired</span> <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span> | Flag to get the campaign spent. <br />Default: <var>false</var>.

<a href="#campaign-resource-properties"><button className="responsePropertiesButton"><Caret />Resource properties</button></a>

</div> <div class="child2">

<TabItem value="TypeScript" label="TypeScript">

See [TypeScript prerequisites](/getting-started/typescript-prerequisites.mdx) for usage. 

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        statusCode: number;
        responseObject: {
          owId: number;
          parentOrganizationName: string;
          id: number;
          uowId: number;
          campaignName: string;
          advertiserDomain: string;
          creativeType: number;
          campaignType: number;
          totalBudgetPacing: boolean;
          isTvAd: boolean;
          budgetDay: number;
          budgetTotal: number;
          maxBid: number;
          timezone: number;
          startTime: number;
          endTime: number;
          status: string;
          dspMargin: number;
          platformMargin: number;
          userDealMargin: number;
          spentScale: boolean;
          creativeIds: string;
          conversionType: string;
          bidOptimization: boolean;
          bidPacing: boolean;
          isBidShading: boolean;
          impressionCapping: number;
          maxDayImpressions: number;
          maxDayClicks: number;
          maxDayConversions: number;
          totalImpressions: number;
          totalClicks: number;
          totalConversions: number;
          deviceType: string;
          trafficType: string;
          exchanges: string;
          isLocationWithOrFilter: boolean;
          countryId: string;
          locationDetails: {}
          isCampaignFromNewPlatform: boolean;
          organizationName: string;
          userEmail: string;
          userName: string;
          conversionTypeId: number;
          isUnapprovedAudienceTargeted: boolean;
          isAllAudienceUnapproved: boolean;
          createDate: number;
          ioId: number;
          ioName: number;
          prebidAudienceSegmentIdList: number[];
          campaignTypeId: number;
          budgetTypeId: number;
          isAdvanceAudioVideoTargeted: boolean;
          isEstimatorAvailable: boolean;
          isEditAccess: boolean;
          isMarginSet: boolean;
          isApprovalAccess: boolean;
          isParentInvoiceTemplateSet: boolean;
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

function getCampaign(): Promise<Responses> {
  const options = {
    method: 'GET',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v2/cmp/campaign/{campaignId}',
    params: {
      query?: {
        isSpentRequired?: `boolean`,
      },
      path: {
        campaignId: `number`
      }
    },
  };
  
  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

</TabItem></Tabs>

### Get list of campaigns

<CopyUrl method="GET" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v2/cmp/campaigns/data" operationId="getCampaignListData" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Returns list of campaigns based on set of filters and <var>conversionId</var> query parameter.

A campaign <var>id</var> can be used to [Get campaign details by ID](#get-campaign-details-by-id) or to perform various actions in [Campaign management](#campaign-management).

| Query parameters |  |
| ---- | --- |
| `searchField` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filter results by search field. |
| `limit` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Number of entries returned per page, default: <var>50</var>. |
| `pageNo` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Page number of retrieved data, default: <var>1</var>. |
| `sortBy` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Sorts by ascending (<var>+</var>) or descending (<var>-</var>), default: <var>-created</var>. | 
| `conversionId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Targeted campaign will be returned at top of list. |
| `status` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filter by multiple statuses. |
| `owIds` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Filter by Organization Workspace IDs. | 

<a href="#campaign-resource-properties"><button className="responsePropertiesButton"><Caret />Resource properties</button></a>

</div><div class="child2">

### Get list of campaigns with basic details

<CopyUrl method="GET" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v2/cmp/campaigns/list" operationId="GetCampaignList" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Returns a paginated list of campaigns with their basic details.

| Query parameters |  |
| ---- | --- |
| `search_field` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Search results by keyword. |
| `no_of_entries` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Maximum number of entries returned, default: <var>50</var>. |
| `pgno` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Page number for the data, default: <var>1</var>. |
| `order` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Sorts by ascending (<var>asc</var>) or descending (<var>desc</var>). | 
| `creative_type_ids` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Filter by [creative type ID](/guidelines/creative-api/#get-creative-types-list).  |
| `created_time_of_creative` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filter by creative creation time. |
| `status` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filter by multiple statuses. <br />Supported values: <var>running</var>, <var>pending</var>, <var>paused</var>, <var>draft</var>, <var>rejected</var>, <var>expired</var>. |
| `ow_ids` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Filter by Organization Workspace IDs. |

### Get basic details of campaigns

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/basic/list" operationId="getCampaignBasicDetailList" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

| Request schema | |
| --- | --- |
| `pageNo` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Page number of retrieved data, default: <var>1</var>. |
| `noOfEntries` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Number of entries returned per page, default: <var>20</var>, maximum: <var>500</var>. |
| `sortBy` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Sorts results by specified field. |
| `order` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Sorts by ascending (<var>asc</var>) or descending (<var>desc</var>). |
| `searchField` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filter results by search field. |
| `campaignIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filters results by provided [campaign IDs](#get-list-of-campaigns). |
| `owIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filters results by provided OW IDs. |
| `status` <br /><span class="type-text">array of strings</span> | Filters results by provided campaign statuses. |
| `responseFields` <br /><span class="type-text">array of strings</span> | Choose which values to return in response, supported: <var>id</var>, <var>campaignId</var>, <var>name</var>,<var>creativeTypeId</var>, <var>status</var>, <var>startTime</var>, <var>endTime</var>, <var>createdAt</var>, <var>modifiedAt</var>, <var>owId</var>, <var>organizationName</var>, <var>organizationLogo</var>. |
| `creativeTypeIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filters results by provided [creative type IDs](/guidelines/creative-api/#get-creative-types-list).  |
| `campaignTypeIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filter results by [campaign type IDs](#get-campaign-count-with-campaign-type). |
| `isAllOwIds` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span> | Filters records with all allowed OW IDs if <var>true</var>, prioritizes records over <var>owIds</var>. |
| `ids` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Prioritizes results of provided campaign group IDs over other records.
| `ioIdsList` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filters results by provided [insertion order IDs](#get-insertion-order-details). |

### Get list of campaigns with filters

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/customer/campaigns/list" operationId="getCampaignDetails" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Filter campaign list by the following parameters:

| Request schema | |
| --- | --- |
| `searchField` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filter results by search field. |
| `noOfEntries` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Number of entries returned per page, default: <var>20</var>, maximum: <var>500</var>. |
| `pageNo` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Page number of retrieved data, default: <var>1</var>. |
| `sortBy` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Sorts by specified field. |
| `order`  <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Sorting order, ascending (<var>asc</var>) or descending (<var>desc</var>). |
| `campaignIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filter results by [campaign IDs](#get-list-of-campaigns). |
| `owIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filter results by Organization Workspace IDs. |
| `status` <br /><span class="type-text">array of strings</span> | Filter results by statuses. |
| `created` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Date created, unix epoch.
| `isBasicInfo` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span> | If <var>true</var> returns fewer details, if <var>false</var> returns all details. 
| `creativeTypeIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filter results by [creative type IDs](/guidelines/creative-api/#get-creative-types-list).  |
| `campaignTypeIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filter results by [campaign type IDs](#get-campaign-count-with-campaign-type). |
| `ids` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Prioritizes results of provided campaign IDs over other records. |

### Get campaign budget details

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v2/cmp/campaign/budgetInfo" operationId="getCampaignBudgetInfo" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Calculates and returns campaign budget and impression information. Derives any missing budget or daily-impression value from the supplied combination of total budget, timezone, daily budget, start/end time, and budget type. 

When a <var>scheduling</var> map is provided, daily amounts are proportionally distributed across scheduled hours only. Pass <var>isGraphInfoRequired=true</var> to include per-day graph data in the response.

| Request schema |  |
| ---- | --- |
| `campaignId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | [Campaign ID](#get-list-of-campaigns) to get budget info for. |
| `budgetTotal` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Campaign total budget. <br />Note: can be omitted if fixed daily budget, start time, and end time are provided. |
| `budgetDay` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Campaign daily budget. <br />Note: can be omitted if fixed daily budget, start time, and end time are provided. |
| `fixedDailyBudget` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span> | Flag to indicate whether daily budget is fixed or not, default: <var>false</var>. |
| `startTime` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> <span class="required-text">required</span> | Campaign start time. | 
| `endTime` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Campaign end time.  <br />Note: can be omitted if fixed daily budget, start time, and end time are provided. |
| `timezone` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> <span class="required-text">required</span> | [Timezone ID](/guidelines/master-api#get-timezones). |
| `budgetTypeId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | [Budget Type ID](#get-list-of-campaign-budget-types) of given campaign (impression-based or dollar-based). |
| `campaignTypeId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | [Campaign Type ID](#get-campaign-count-with-campaign-type), default: <var>1</var> (Advanced). |
| `isGraphInfoRequired` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span> | Include per-day `graphInfo` data in the response: <var>true</var>. |
| `scheduling` <br /><span class="type-text">object</span> | Map of weekday index (<var>0</var>–<var>6</var>) to an array of <var>[startTime, endTime]</var> string ranges campaign delivery is scheduled for that day. |
| `maxBid` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Maximum allowed bid price, used for impression-based PG campaigns. |
| `totalImpressions` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Targeted total impressions, used for impression-based PG campaigns. |
| `dailyImpressions` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Targeted daily impressions, used for impression-based campaigns. |

<details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem"}}>
    <summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>Response properties</summary>
    <table className="objectProperties">
    <tr>

    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`budgetTotal` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Campaign total budget.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`actualSpent` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Amount spent by the campaign so far.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`budgetDay` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Campaign daily budget.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`fixedDailyBudget` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span></td>
    <td>Whether daily budget is fixed.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`startTime` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Campaign start time.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`endTime` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Campaign end time.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>

    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`budgetTypeId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>[Budget Type ID](#get-list-of-campaign-budget-types) of given campaign (impression-based or dollar-based).</td>
    </tr>

    <tr>

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        statusCode: number;
        responseObject: {
          campaignId?: number;
          budgetTotal: number;
          actualSpent: number;
          budgetDay: number;
          fixedDailyBudget: boolean;
          startTime: number;
          endTime: number;
          timezone: number;
          budgetTypeId: number;
          dailyImpressions?: number;
          actualImpressionSpent?: number;
          totalImpressions?: number;
          maxBid?: number;
          spendingBudget?: number;
          pgFeesBudget?: number;
          graphInfo?: {
            date: string;
            amount: number;
            type: string;
          }[];
        };
      };
    };
  };
}

function getCampaignBudgetInfo(): Promise<Responses> {
  const options = {
    method: 'POST',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v2/cmp/campaign/budgetInfo',
    requestBody: {
      content: {
        "application/json": {
          campaignId: `number`,
          budgetTotal: `number`,
          budgetDay: `number`,
          fixedDailyBudget: `boolean`,
          startTime: `number`,
          endTime: `number`,
          timezone: `number`,
          budgetTypeId: `number`,
          campaignTypeId: `number`,
          isGraphInfoRequired: `boolean`,
          scheduling: `object`,
          maxBid: `number`,
          totalImpressions: `number`,
          dailyImpressions: `number`,
        }
      }
    }
  };
  
  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

### Get list of campaign groups

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/campaigngroup/list" operationId="getCampaignGroupList" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Filter campaign list by the following parameters:

| Request schema | |
| --- | --- |
| `searchField` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filter results by search field. |
| `noOfEntries` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Number of entries returned per page, default: <var>20</var>, maximum: <var>500</var>. |
| `pageNo` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Page number of retrieved data, default: <var>1</var>. |
| `sortBy` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Sorts by specified field. |
| `order`  <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Sorting order, ascending (<var>asc</var>) or descending (<var>desc</var>). |
| `campaignGroupIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filter results by campaign group IDs. |
| `owIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filter results by Organization Workspace IDs. |
| `isAllOwIds` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span> | Filters records with all allowed OW IDs if <var>true</var>, prioritizes records over <var>owIds</var>. |
| `ids` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Prioritizes results of provided campaign group IDs over other records. |

</div><div class="child2">

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        success: boolean;
        data: {
          data: {
            groupId: number;
            groupName: string;
            createdDate: number;
            owId: number;
          }[]
          totalRecords: number;
          filteredRecords: number
        }
      }
    };

  400: {

        }[]
      }
    };
  };
}

function getCampaignGroupList(): Promise<Responses> {

    method: 'POST',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/campaigngroup/list',
    requestBody: {
      content: {
        "application/json": {
          searchField?: `string`,
          noOfEntries?: `number`,
          pageNo?: `number`,
          sortBy?: `string`,
          order?: `string`,
          campaignGroupIds?: `array of numbers`,
          owIds?: `array of numbers`,
          isAllOwIds?: `boolean`,
          ids?: `array of numbers`
        }
      }
    }
  };
  
  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

### Get campaign count by status

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/campaigns/count" operationId="getCampaignsCount" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

This API returns a count of campaigns for each status type.


| Request schema |  |
| ---- | --- |
| `owIds` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Comma separated Organization Workspace IDs, returns campaign counts (supported: <var>all</var>).
| `ioIds`<br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Comma separated [insertion order IDs](#get-insertion-order-details), returns campaign counts (with <var>owIds</var> = <var>all</var>).
| `campaignTypeIds` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filter results by [campaign type IDs](#get-campaign-count-with-campaign-type) (<var>ioIds</var> also required).
| `campaignExpiredAfterEpoch` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Unix epoch timestamp (in milliseconds) expiration.
| `searchField` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Returns campaign counts for field matched campaign ID or Name. |

<details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem"}}>
    <summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>Response properties</summary>
    <table className="objectProperties">
    <tr>
    <td>`order` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Display order.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`status_key` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>Status key.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`status_label` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>Status label.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`status_count` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Campaign count.</td>
    </tr>
    </table>
    </details>

### Get campaign count with campaign type

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/campaign-type/count" operationId="getCampaignsCountWithCampaignType" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Returns values of campaign types with count of campaigns. 

| Request schema |  |
| ---- | --- |
| `budgetTypeIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filters results for selected [budget type ID](#get-list-of-campaign-budget-types): dollar-based campaign or impression-based campaign. |
| `statusList` <br /><span class="type-text">array of strings</span> | Filters results for types of campaign statuses. |
| `countRequired` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span> | Returns campaigns count with campaign type if <var>true</var>. |
| `campaignIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filters results for selected [campaign IDs](#get-list-of-campaigns). |
| `ioIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filters results for selected [insertion order IDs](#get-insertion-order-details). |
| `creativeTypeIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filters results for [creative type IDs](/guidelines/creative-api/#get-creative-types-list).  |

| Properties | |
| --- | --- |
| `Advanced` <br /><span class="type-text">ID: 1</span> | Campaign with audience, impressions, and bid optimization settings. |
| `PG` <br /><span class="type-text">ID: 2</span> | Campaign associated with PG deal. |

### Get campaign count by creative type

<CopyUrl method="GET" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v2/cmp/campaigns/count" operationId="CampaignCountbycreativetype" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Get a count of campaigns by creative type ID and given Organization Workspace IDs.

| Query parameters | |
| --- | --- |
| `sortType` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Sort by ascending (<var>asc</var>) or descending (<var>desc</var>). |
| `creativeTypeIds` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Comma separated [creative type IDs](/guidelines/creative-api/#get-creative-types-list), supported values: <br />Image: <var>11</var>. <br />HTML: <var>13</var>. <br />Video: <var>14</var>. <br />Ticker Banner: <var>16</var>. <br />Audio: <var>17</var>. <br />GOTV: <var>18</var>. |
| `searchField` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filter results by search field. |
| `owIds` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Filter results by comma separated Organization Workspace IDs. |

<details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem"}}>
    <summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>Response properties</summary>
    <table className="objectProperties">
    <tr>

    </tr>

    <td>Status key.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`status_label` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>Status label.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`status_count` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Campaign count.</td>
    </tr>
    </table>
    </details>

### Get creative type and campaigns count

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/creative-types/count" operationId="getCampaignCountWithCreativeTypeByIoIds" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

| Request schema | |

| `owIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filters results by Organization Workspace IDs. |
| `ioIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filters results by [insertion order IDs](#get-insertion-order-details). |
| `status` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filters results based on list of statuses, pass empty string to filter for all statuses. |
| `statusList` <br /><span class="type-text">array of strings</span> | Filters results for types of campaign statuses.

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        success: boolean;
        data: {
          count: number;
          creativeType: number;
          creativeTypeId: number;
        }[]
      }
    };
  };
  422: {
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        success: boolean;
        errorObjects: {
          error: string
        }[]
      }
    };
  };
}

function getCampaignCountWithCreativeTypeByIoIds(): Promise<Responses> {
  const options = {
    method: 'POST',

        "application/json": {
          ioIds?: `array of numbers`,
          owIds?: `array of numbers`,
          status?: `string`,
          statusList?: `array of strings`
        }
      }
    }
  };
  
  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

### Get basic conversion list

<CopyUrl method="GET" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/conversion/basic/list" operationId="getBasicConversionDetailsListByOwId" />

<div class="endpoint-container">

Get a basic list of conversion details. See the [Conversions API](/guidelines/conversion-api/) for more details.

| `owId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Organization Workspace ID. |
| `conversionTypeId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Filter results by conversion Type ID:<br /><var>1</var>: Pixel. <br /><var>2</var>: Postback. |
| `searchField` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filter results by search field. |
| `sortBy` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Sorts by ascending (<var>+</var>) or descending (<var>-</var>), default: <var>-created</var>. |
| `limit` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Number of entries returned per page, default: <var>20</var>. |
| `pageNo` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Page number of retrieved data, default: <var>1</var>. |

### Get list of campaign start dates or end dates

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/startDate/list" operationId="getCampaignStartDateList" />

<br /><CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/endDate/list" operationId="getCampaignEndDateList" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Get a paginated list of campaign's start dates with supported filters.

| Request schema | |
| --- | --- |
| `pageNo` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Page number of retrieved data, default: <var>1</var>. |
| `noOfEntries` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Number of entries returned per page, default: <var>300</var>. |
| `sortBy` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Sorts by ascending (<var>+</var>) or descending (<var>-</var>), default: <var>-campaignDate</var>. |
| `startDate` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filter results by campaign start date, only returns results that have a start date greater than the specified date. |
| `endDate` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filter results by campaign end date, only returns results that have an end date less than the specified date. |
| `ids` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Prioritizes selected campaigns over other dates in response. |
| `isAllOwIds` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span> | Filter results with all allowed OW IDs, if <var>true</var> prioritizes over <var>owIds</var>. |
| `owIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filters for specified Customer OW IDs. |

<details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem"}}>
    <summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>Response properties</summary>
    <table className="objectProperties">
    <tr>
    <td>`data` <br /><span class="type-text">array of strings</span></td>
    <td>List of dates.</td>
    </tr>
    </table>
    </details>

</div><div class="child2">

### Get campaign report data

<CopyUrl method="GET" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/campaigns/report/data" operationId="GetCampaignReportData" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Get report data by campaign IDs and other filters. 

| Query parameters |  |
| ---- | --- |
| `searchField` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Search results by ID or name. |
| `limit` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Number of entries returned per page, default: <var>10</var>. |
| `pageNo` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Page number of retrieved data, default: <var>1</var>. |
| `sortBy` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Sorts by ascending (<var>+</var>) or descending (<var>-</var>), default: <var>-created</var>. <br />Supported values: <var>created</var>, <var>name</var>, <var>id</var>, <var>status</var>, <var>modifiedDate</var>. |
| `status` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filter results by the following status values: <var>running</var>, <var>paused</var>, <var>pending</var>, <var>expired</var>, <var>draft</var>. |
| `conversionId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Filter results by conversion ID. |
| `campaignIds` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Filter results by comma separated [campaign IDs](#get-list-of-campaigns). |

## Campaign management

Create, update and manage campaigns.

### Create new campaign

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v2/cmp/campaigns/add" operationId="saveCampaign" />
<br /><CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v2/cmp/campaigns/draft/add" operationId="saveDraftCampaign" />


<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Create a new campaign in <var>pending</var> or <var>draft</var> status.

<details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem"}}>
    <summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>Request schema</summary>
    <table className="objectProperties">
    <tr>
    <td>`campaignName` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>Campaign name.</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
    <td>`isAgreementChecked` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span></td>
    <td>Check to proceed: <var>true</var>.</td>
    </tr>
     <tr>
    <td>`ioId` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>[Insertion order ID](#get-insertion-order-details) that campaign belongs to.</td>
    </tr>
              <tr>
    <td>`countryId` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>[Country ID](/guidelines/master-api#get-country-segment).</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
    <td>`advertiserDomain` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>Advertiser domain.</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
    <td>`creativeType` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>[Creative type ID](/guidelines/creative-api/#get-creative-types-list).</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
    <td>`campaignType` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>[Campaign type ID](#get-campaign-count-with-campaign-type).</td>
    </tr>
            <tr>
    <td>`startTime` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Campaign start time.</td>
    </tr>
                <tr>
    <td>`endTime` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Campaign end time (required if total budget pacing is enabled).</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
    <td>`totalBudgetPacing` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span></td>
    <td>Budget is spent equally every hour: <var>true</var>.</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
    <td>`budgetDay` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Daily budget for campaign serving daily (not applicable if budget pacing is set to <var>true</var>).</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
    <td>`budgetTotal` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Total budget for campaign serving period.</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
    <td>`budgetTypeId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>[Budget type ID](/guidelines/campaign-api#get-list-of-campaign-budget-types).</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
    <td>`maxBid` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Max bid for each bid request.</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
    <td>`minBid` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> <span class="required-text">optional</span></td>
    <td>Minimum bid for each bid request.</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
    <td>`allowedMinBid` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> <span class="required-text">optional</span></td>
    <td>Server-enforced minimum bid floor allowed for this campaign.</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
    <td>`totalCPM` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> <span class="required-text">optional</span></td>
    <td>Total CPM for the campaign.</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
    <td>`thirdPartyCost` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> <span class="required-text">optional</span></td>
    <td>Campaign-level 3rd-party cost override, between <var>0</var> and <var>999</var>. Omit to keep the account-level cost applied. Only honored when 3rd-party cost is active and campaign-level editing is enabled for the Advertiser's workspace.</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
    <td>`timezone` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>[Timezone ID](/guidelines/master-api#get-timezones) for campaign.</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
    <td>`creativeIds` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>Comma separated [creative IDs](/guidelines/creative-api#get-list-of-creatives-and-details).</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
    <td>`bidOptimization` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span> <span class="required-text">optional</span></td>
    <td>Optimize bid price based on analysis: <var>true</var>. </td>
    </tr>
            <tr>
    <td>`bidPacing` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span> <span class="required-text">optional</span></td>
    <td>Budget is spent equally every hour: <var>true</var>. </td>
    </tr>
            <tr>
    <td>`impressionCapping` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> <span class="required-text">optional</span></td>
    <td>Maximum impressions in one inventory. </td>
    </tr>
            <tr>
    <td>`frequencyCapEnabled` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span> <span class="required-text">optional</span></td>
    <td>Enables frequency capping via <var>frequencyCapping</var>: <var>true</var>.</td>
    </tr>
            <tr>
    <td>`frequencyCapping` <br /><span class="type-text">object</span> <span class="required-text">optional</span></td>
    <td>Map of [frequency cap type ID](#get-frequency-cap-types) to cap duration/value, keyed by the recurrence period the cap applies over: <var>1</var> = Day(s), <var>2</var> = Week(s), <var>3</var> = Month(s), <var>4</var> = Campaign duration.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td colSpan="2">
        <details className="objectPropertiesDetails">
        <summary>`frequencyCapping` entry properties</summary>
        <table className="objectProperties">
        <tr>
        <td>`frequencyCapDuration` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
        <td>Number of days/weeks/months the cap spans (per the key's type); must be <var>0</var> for the campaign Duration type (id <var>4</var>).</td>
        </tr>
        <tr>
        <td>`frequencyCapValue` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
        <td>Maximum impressions allowed within `frequencyCapDuration`.</td>
        </tr>
        </table></details>
        </td>
        <td></td>
      </tr>
            <tr>
    <td>`maxDayClicks` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> <span class="required-text">optional</span></td>
    <td>Maximum daily clicks. </td>
    </tr>
            <tr>
    <td>`totalClicks` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> <span class="required-text">optional</span></td>
    <td>Total clicks. </td>
    </tr>
            <tr>
    <td>`maxDayImpressions` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> <span class="required-text">optional</span></td>
    <td>Maximum daily impressions. </td>
    </tr>
            <tr>
    <td>`totalImpressions` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> <span class="required-text">optional</span></td>

    </tr>

    </tr>
            <tr>
    <td>`totalConversions` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> <span class="required-text">optional</span></td>

    <tr>

    </tr>

    <td>`creativeAdvanceTargeting` <br /><span class="type-text">JSON</span> <span class="required-text">optional</span></td>

    <td>`campaignEstimatorMetaData` <br /><span class="type-text">JSON</span> <span class="required-text">optional</span></td>

    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`campaignOptimization` <br /><span class="type-text">object</span> <span class="required-text">optional</span></td>
    <td>Campaign optimization settings for Goal-AI. <br />Required when `campaignOptimizationType` is <var>ai_based</var>.</td>
    </tr>

      <td colSpan="2">

        <td>`aiGoal` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>

        <tr>
        <td>`aiTargetKpi` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>

        </tr>
        <tr>
        <td>`aiConversionId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
        <td>Conversion ID for AI optimization.</td>
        </tr>
        </table></details>
        </td>
        <td></td>
      </tr>

<TabItem value="TypeScript" label="TypeScript">

See [TypeScript prerequisites](/getting-started/typescript-prerequisites.mdx) for usage. 

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    headers: {
      [name: string]: unknown;
    };
      content: {
      "application/json": {
        statusCode: number;
        responseObject: {
          data: number;
          message: string;
          status: string;
        }
      }
    };
  };
}

function saveCampaign(): Promise<Responses> {
/*
* or function saveDraftCampaign(): Promise<Responses> {
*/
  const options = {
    method: 'POST',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v2/cmp/campaigns/add',
/*
* or url : 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v2/cmp/campaigns/draft/add',
*/
    requestBody: {
         content: {
        "application/json": {
          advertiserId?: `number`,
          dspId?: `number`,
          owId?: `number`,
          uowId?: `number`,
          id?: `number`,
          campaignName?: `string`,
          advertiserDomain?: `string`,
          creativeType?: `number`,
          campaignType?: `number`,
          totalBudgetPacing?: `boolean`,
          budgetDay?: `number`,
          budgetTotal?: `number`,
          maxBid?: `number`,
          timezone?: `number`,
          startTime?: `number`,
          endTime?: `number`,
          status?: `string`,
          created?: `number`,
          excludeFromPlatformServing?: `boolean`,
          forTest?: `boolean`,
          modifiedDate?: `string`,
          dspMarginJson?: `string`,
          platformMarginJson?: `string`,
          dspMargin?: `number`,
          platformMargin?: `number`,
          userDealMargin?: `number`,
          isAgreementChecked?: `boolean`,
          spentScale?: `boolean`,
          creativeIds?: `string`,
          targetCPI?: `number`,
          conversionType?: `string`,
          conversionTypeId?: `number`,
          appURL?: `string`,
          bidOptimization?: `boolean`,
          bidPacing?: `boolean`,
          impressionCapping?: `number`,
          maxDayImpressions?: `number`,
          maxDayClicks?: `number`,
          maxDayConversions?: `number`,
          totalImpressions?: `number`,
          totalClicks?: `number`,
          totalConversions?: `number`,
          bidStrategyFlag?: `boolean`,
          ioId?: `number`,
          prebidAudienceSegmentIdList?: `array of numbers`,
          isBidShading?: `boolean`,
          carriers?: `string`,
          networkType?: `string`,
          deviceType?: `string`,
          trafficType?: `string`,
          manufacturer?: `string`,
          device?: `string`,
          os?: `string`,
          osVersion?: `string`,
          technologyFlag?: `boolean`,
          exchanges: `string`,
          advancedFlag?: `boolean`,
          userDealId?: `string`,
          groupDealId?: `string`,
          publisherAdCategory: `string`,
          campaignIabCategoryIds?: `string`,
          stateIds?: `string`,
          countryId?: `number`,
          dmaIds?: `string`,
          zipcodes?: `string`,
          locationFileIds?: `string`,
          inventoryKeywords?: `array of strings`,
          inventoryUrls?: `array of strings`,
          pmpDealIds?: `array of numbers`,
          politicalAdvertiserClientId?: `number`,
          scheduling?: {
            [key: `string`]: `Record<string, never>`,
          },
          ageRangeIds?: `string`,
          genderIds?: `string`,
          languageIds?: `string`,
          incomeRangeIds?: `string`,
          ethnicityIds?: `string`,
          interestIds?: `string`,
          demographicTargetingFlag?: `boolean`,
          conversionIds?: `string`,
          creativesPlacementMapping?: {
            [key: `string`]: `number`,
          },
          isTvAd?: `boolean`,
          creativeAdvanceTargeting?: {
            [key: `string`]: `array of numbers`
          },
          budgetTypeId?: `number`,
          campaignEstimatorMetaData?: {
            reachMeta?: {
              [key: string]: Record<string, never>;
            };
            landScapeMeta?: {
              [key: string]: Record<string, never>;
            };
            sliderMeta?: {
              [key: string]: Record<string, never>;
            },
          },
          customAudienceTargeted?: `boolean`,
          advanceAudioVideoTargeted?: `boolean`,
          isAdvanceAudioVideoTargeted?: `boolean`,
        };
      };
    }
  };

  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

---

### Update campaign

<CopyUrl method="PATCH" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v2/cmp/campaign/{campaignId}" operationId="editCampaignPatch" />
<br /><CopyUrl method="PUT" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v2/cmp/campaign/draft/{campaignId}" operationId="editDraftCampaign" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Update a campaign's properties.

When updating an impression-based campaign that has never had <var>totalImpressions</var> set (most commonly, one still in draft), <var>budgetTotal</var> and <var>totalImpressions</var> are required. For a dollar-based campaign, provide the applicable budget value. For an impression-based campaign, provide the applicable impressions value. Otherwise provide <var>0</var> for the respective field.

:::info

If campaign is in <var>draft</var> status, this API will automatically update status from <var>draft</var> to <var>pending</var>. In all other cases status will not be updated.

:::

| Path parameters | |
| --- | --- |
| `campaignId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | [Campaign ID](#get-list-of-campaigns). |

<td>To indicate if the advance targeting is enabled if this is true, 'creativeAdvanceTargeting' is optional and it is assumed that every video creative segment is targeted.</td>

<td>`isBidShading` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span></td>
<td>To indicate if the bid shading is enabled.</td>
</tr>
<tr>

<td>String to Integer Map of creative advanced targeting group to list of creative advance targeting segment IDs.</td>
</tr>

</tr>

</tr>
<tr>
<td>`campaignOptimization` <br /><span class="type-text">object</span></td>
<td>Campaign optimization settings for Goal-AI. <br />Required when `campaignOptimizationType` is <var>ai_based</var>.</td>
</tr>

    <table className="objectProperties">

    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`aiConversionId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Conversion ID for AI optimization.</td>
    </tr>
    </table></details>
  </td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>`inventoryKeywords` <br /><span class="type-text">array of strings</span></td>
<td>Keywords to target inventory by contextual match, e.g. <var>Clothing</var>, <var>Education</var>.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>`inventoryUrls` <br /><span class="type-text">array of strings</span></td>
<td>URLs of specific inventory placements to target.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>`pmpDealIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span></td>
<td>[PMP deal IDs](/guidelines/inventory-api#get-pmp-deals-list) to assign/de-assign to/from campaigns.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>`creativesPlacementMapping` <br /><span class="type-text">object</span></td>
<td>Creative to placement mapping.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td>`isTvAd` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span></td>
<td>Indicates the campaign is a TV ad (OTT/CTV buying): <var>true</var>. When enabled, `creativeType` must be video, and `deviceType`/`trafficType` must be Connected TV, OTT, and CTV; advanced creative targeting can't be applied.</td>
</tr>

<TabItem value="TypeScript" label="TypeScript">

See [TypeScript prerequisites](/getting-started/typescript-prerequisites.mdx) for usage.

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    headers: {
      [name: string]: unknown;
    };
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        statusCode: number;
        responseObject: {
          data: number;
          message: string;
          status: string;
        }
      }
    };
  };
}

function editCampaignPatch(): Promise<Responses> {
  const options = {
    method: 'PATCH',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v2/cmp/campaign/{campaignId}',
    params: {
      path: {
        campaignId: `number`
      }
    },
    requestBody: {
              content: {
        "application/json": {
          advertiserId?: `number`,
          dspId?: `number`,
          owId?: `number`,
          uowId?: `number`,
          id?: `number`,
          campaignName?: `string`,
          advertiserDomain?: `string`,
          creativeType?: `number`,
          campaignType?: `number`,
          totalBudgetPacing?: `boolean`,
          budgetDay?: `number`,
          budgetTotal?: `number`,
          maxBid?: `number`,
          timezone?: `number`,
          startTime?: `number`,
          endTime?: `number`,
          status?: `string`,
          created?: `number`,
          excludeFromPlatformServing?: `boolean`,
          forTest?: `boolean`,
          modifiedDate?: `string`,
          dspMarginJson?: `string`,
          platformMarginJson?: `string`,
          dspMargin?: `number`,
          platformMargin?: `number`,
          userDealMargin?: `number`,
          isAgreementChecked?: `boolean`,
          spentScale?: `boolean`,
          creativeIds?: `string`,
          targetCPI?: `number`,
          conversionType?: `string`,
          conversionTypeId?: `number`,
          appURL?: `string`,
          bidOptimization?: `boolean`,
          bidPacing?: `boolean`,
          impressionCapping?: `number`,
          maxDayImpressions?: `number`,
          maxDayClicks?: `number`,
          maxDayConversions?: `number`,
          totalImpressions?: `number`,
          totalClicks?: `number`,
          totalConversions?: `number`,
          bidStrategyFlag?: `boolean`,
          ioId?: `number`,
          prebidAudienceSegmentIdList?: `array of numbers`,
          isBidShading?: `boolean`,
          carriers?: `string`,
          networkType?: `string`,
          deviceType?: `string`,
          trafficType?: `string`,
          manufacturer?: `string`,
          device?: `string`,
          os?: `string`,
          osVersion?: `string`,
          technologyFlag?: `boolean`,
          exchanges: `string`,
          advancedFlag?: `boolean`,
          userDealId?: `string`,
          groupDealId?: `string`,
          publisherAdCategory: `string`,
          campaignIabCategoryIds?: `string`,
          stateIds?: `string`,
          countryId?: `number`,
          dmaIds?: `string`,
          zipcodes?: `string`,
          locationFileIds?: `string`,
          inventoryKeywords?: `array of strings`,
          inventoryUrls?: `array of strings`,
          pmpDealIds?: `array of numbers`,
          politicalAdvertiserClientId?: `number`,
          scheduling?: {
            [key: `string`]: `Record<string, never>`,
          },
          ageRangeIds?: `string`,
          genderIds?: `string`,
          languageIds?: `string`,
          incomeRangeIds?: `string`,
          ethnicityIds?: `string`,
          interestIds?: `string`,
          demographicTargetingFlag?: `boolean`,
          conversionIds?: `string`,
          creativesPlacementMapping?: {
            [key: `string`]: `number`,
          },
          isTvAd?: `boolean`,
          creativeAdvanceTargeting?: {
            [key: `string`]: `array of numbers`
          },
          budgetTypeId?: `number`,
          campaignEstimatorMetaData?: {
            reachMeta?: {
              [key: string]: Record<string, never>;
            };
            landScapeMeta?: {
              [key: string]: Record<string, never>;
            };
            sliderMeta?: {
              [key: string]: Record<string, never>;
            },
          },
          customAudienceTargeted?: `boolean`,
          advanceAudioVideoTargeted?: `boolean`,
          isAdvanceAudioVideoTargeted?: `boolean`,
        };
      };
    };
  };
  
  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

</TabItem></Tabs>

</div></div>

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        success: boolean;
        data: {
          message: string;
          campaignId: number
        }
      }
    };
  };
  422: {
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        success: boolean;
        errorObjects: {
          error: string
        }
      }
    };
  };
}

function addPGCampaign(): Promise<Responses> {
/*
*or 
*
*function saveDraftCampaign(): Promise<Responses> {
*/
  const options = {
    method: 'POST',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/pg/campaigns/add',
/*
*or
*
*   url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v2/cmp/campaigns/draft/add',
*/
    requestBody: {
      content: {
        "application/json": {
          countryId?: `number`,
          politicalAdvertiserClientId?: `number`,
          conversionTargeting?: {
            conversionTypeId?: `number`,
            conversionIds: `array of numbers`,
          },
          creativeTargeting: {
            creativeTypeId: `number`,
            creativeIds: `array of numbers`,
          },
          inventoryTargeting: {
            pgDealIds: `array of numbers`,
            paymentTypeId?: `number`,
          },
          pgCampaignInfo: {
            campaignName: `string`,
            timeZoneId: `number`,
            spendingBudget?: `number`,
            maxBid: `number`,
            startTime: `number`,
            endTime?: `number`,
            totalImpressions?: `number`,
            advertiserDomain: `string`,
            ioId: `number`,
            budgetTypeId: `number`,
            campaignTypeId?: `number`,
            owId?: `number`,
            uowId?: `number`,
            creativeTypeId?: `number`,
            status?: `string`,
            configObj?: `string`,
            totalBudget?: `number`,
            pgFeesPercentage?: `number`,
          }
        }
      }
    }
  };
  
  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        success: boolean;
        data: {
          message: string;
          campaignId: number
        }
      }
    };
  };
  422: {
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        success: boolean;
        errorObjects: {
          error: string
        }
      }
    };
  };
}

function editPGCampaign(): Promise<Responses> {
/*
*or 
*
*function editDraftPGCampaign(): Promise<Responses> {
*/
  const options = {
    method: 'PATCH',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/pg/campaigns/{campaignId}',
/*
*or
*
*   url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v2/cmp/campaigns/draft/{campaignId}',
*/
    params: {
      path: {
        campaignId: `number`
      }
    },
    requestBody: {
      content: {
        "application/json": {
          countryId?: `number`,
          politicalAdvertiserClientId?: `number`,
          conversionTargeting?: {
            conversionTypeId?: `number`,
            conversionIds: `array of numbers`,
          },
          creativeTargeting: {
            creativeTypeId: `number`,
            creativeIds: `array of numbers`,
          },
          inventoryTargeting: {
            pgDealIds: `array of numbers`,
            paymentTypeId?: `number`,
          },
          pgCampaignInfo: {
            campaignName: `string`,
            timeZoneId: `number`,
            spendingBudget?: `number`,
            maxBid: `number`,
            startTime: `number`,
            endTime?: `number`,
            totalImpressions?: `number`,
            advertiserDomain: `string`,
            ioId: `number`,
            budgetTypeId: `number`,
            campaignTypeId?: `number`,
            owId?: `number`,
            uowId?: `number`,
            creativeTypeId?: `number`,
            status?: `string`,
            configObj?: `string`,
            totalBudget?: `number`,
            pgFeesPercentage?: `number`,
          }
        }
      }
    }
  };
  
  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

<TabItem value="TypeScript" label="TypeScript">

See [TypeScript prerequisites](/getting-started/typescript-prerequisites.mdx) for usage.

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    content: {
      "application/json;charset=UTF-8": {
        success: boolean;
        data: {
          message: string;
        };
      };
    };
  };
}

function UpdateCampaignName(): Promise<Responses> {
  const options = {
    method: 'PATCH',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/campaign/update-name/{campaign_id}',
    params: {
      path: {
        campaign_id: `string`
      }
    },
    requestBody: {
      content: {
        "application/json": {
          campaignName: `string`,
        }
      }
    }
  };
  
  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

</TabItem></Tabs>

</div></div>

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        statusCode: number;
        responseObject: {
          status: boolean;
        };
      };
    };
  };
}

function Campaignenddateupdate(): Promise<Responses> {
  const options = {
    method: 'PUT',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v2/cmp/campaigns/update-end-date',
    params: {
      header: {
        "X-IAA-HOST": `string`
      }
    },
    requestBody: {
      content: {
        "application/json": {
          endDate: `number`,
          campaignIds: `number`
        }
      }
    }
  };
  
  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

### Change campaign budget

<CopyUrl method="PUT" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v2/cmp/campaigns/update-budget" operationId="updateCampaignBudget" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Update the total budget, daily budget, and max bid of multiple campaigns.


| Request schema |  |
| ----  | --- |
| `campaignIds` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Comma separated [campaign IDs](#get-list-of-campaigns). |
| `maxBid` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Max bid of campaign.
| `totalBudget` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Total budget of campaign.
| `dailyBudget` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span>| Daily Budget of campaign.
| `totalBudgetUpdateType` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Used in the case of a total budget update. There are three possibilities for this parameter. <br /><var>change</var>: Replace total budget with given value.<br /><var>addition</var>: Add budget to the current total budget.<br /><var>distribution</var>: Distribute given total budget in selected campaigns equally.<br />Default value: <var>change</var>. |

<details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem"}}>
    <summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>Response properties</summary>
    <table className="objectProperties">
        <tr>
    <td>`reason` <br /><span class="type-text">object</span></td>
    <td>Reason details.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
      <td colSpan="2">
        <details className="objectPropertiesDetails">
        <summary>`reason` object properties</summary>
        <table className="objectProperties">
        <tr>
        <td>`errorMessage` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
        <td>Error message.</td>
        </tr>
          <tr>
        <td>`id` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
        <td>Campaign ID.</td>
        </tr>
          <tr>
        <td>`campaignName` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
        <td>Campaign name.</td>
        </tr>
        </table></details>
        </td>
        <td></td>
      </tr>
              <tr>
    <td>`modified_data` <br /><span class="type-text">object</span></td>
    <td>Modified data details.<br />See Request schema for supported values.</td>
    </tr>
            <tr>
    <td>`status` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span></td>
    <td>Success status.</td>
    </tr>
    </table>
    </details>

</div>
<div class="child2">

```json title="Request sample"
}
```

```json title="Response 200"
                "errorMessage": "max bid cannot be less than minimum bid 3.0",

}
```

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        statusCode: number;
        responseObject: {
          reason: {
            errorMessage: string;
            id: string;
            campaignName: string;
          }[];
          modified_data: {
            campaingId: number;
            maxBid: number;
            totalBudget: number;
            dailyBudget: number;
            endDate: number;
          }[];
          status: boolean;
        };
      };
    };
  };
}

function Campaignbudgetupdate(): Promise<Responses> {
  const options = {
    method: 'PUT',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v2/cmp/campaigns/update-budget',
    params: {
      header: {
        "X-IAA-HOST": `string`
      }
    },
    requestBody: {
      content: {
        "application/json": {
          totalBudgetUpdateType: `string`,
          campaignIds: `number`,
          dailyBudget: `number`,
          maxBid: `number`,
          totalBudget: `number`
        }
      }
    }
  };
  
  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

### Get campaign cost details

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/campaign/cost" operationId="getCostDetails" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Retrieve cost details for a specific campaign. The request body must include either a <var>campaignId</var> or a combination of <var>audienceIds</var>, <var>creativeIds</var>, and <var>preBidSegmentIds</var>.

| Request schema | |
| --- | --- |
| `campaignId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | [Campaign ID](#get-list-of-campaigns) to get cost details for. |
| `audienceIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | [Audience IDs](/guidelines/audience-api/#audience-details-list) (required if no <var>campaignId</var>). |
| `creativeIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | [Creative IDs](/guidelines/creative-api#get-list-of-creatives-and-details) (required if no <var>campaignId</var>). |
| `preBidSegmentIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | [Pre-bid segment IDs](/guidelines/audience-api/#get-pre-bid-provider-child-segment-details) (required if no <var>campaignId</var>). |
| `creativeTypeId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | [Creative type ID](/guidelines/creative-api#get-creative-types-list). |

  <details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem" }}>
    <summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>Response properties</summary>
    <table className="objectProperties">
    <tr>
    <td>`maxBidPrice` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Maximum bid price.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`adServingCost` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Ad serving cost.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`dataCost` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Data cost.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`preBidCost` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Pre-bid cost.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`msaExistsWith` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>MSA partner name.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`logoURL` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>Partner logo URL.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`thirdPartyCost` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Campaign-level 3rd-party cost, either the Advertiser's override or the account-level cost when no override is set.</td>
    </tr>
    </table>
    </details>

### Get audience reach estimation

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/campaign/estimation/reach" operationId="getAudienceReachEstimation" />

<div class="endpoint-container">

Get audience reach estimation based on targeting parameters. Request body supports dimensions available with `true` value from [Get estimator dimension list](#get-estimator-dimension-list).

| Request schema | |

| `stateIds` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Comma separated [state IDs](/guidelines/master-api#get-state-segment). |

| `whitelistZipCodes` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Comma separated [ZIP codes](/guidelines/master-api/#get-zip-codes-and-state-ids) to include. |
| `ethnicityIds` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Comma separated [ethnicity IDs](/guidelines/master-api#get-ethnicity-segment). |
| `ageIds` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Comma separated [age range IDs](/guidelines/master-api#get-age-segment). |
| `incomeIds` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Comma separated [income range IDs](/guidelines/master-api#get-income-range-segment). |
| `bidPacing` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span> | Enable bid pacing. |
| `budgetDay` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Daily budget. |
| `creativesPlacementMapping` <br /><span class="type-text">object</span> | Creative to placement mapping. |

  <details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem" }}>

    <td>Maximum estimated reach.</td>

```ts
import {
```

        <tr>
    <td>`success` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span></td>
    <td>Indicates email  was sent succesfully: <var>true</var>.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`message` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>Success message.</td>
    </tr>
    </table>
    </details>

</div><div class="child2">

<Tabs>
<TabItem value="JSON" label="JSON" default>

```json title="Response 200"
{
  "success": true,
  "data": "Email reminder resend successfully to set invoice template."
}
```

</TabItem>
<TabItem value="TypeScript" label="TypeScript">

See [TypeScript prerequisites](/getting-started/typescript-prerequisites.mdx) for usage. 

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    headers: {
      [name: string]: unknown;
    };
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        success: boolean;
        data: string;
      }
    }
  };
}

function Resendemailremindertosetinvoicetemplate(): Promise<Responses> {
  const options = {
    method: 'POST',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/email-reminder/set-invoice-template',
    requestBody: {
      content: {
        "application/json": {
          owId: `number`,
        }
      }
    }
  };

  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

</TabItem></Tabs>

</div></div>

## Campaign templates

Manage reusable campaign templates for creating campaigns with pre-configured targeting, creative, and inventory settings.

### Get list of campaign templates

<CopyUrl method="GET" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/template/basic/list" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Get a list of campaign templates with basic details such as ID, name, creation date, and creative type.

| Query parameters | |
| --- | --- |
| `searchField` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Search campaign templates by name or ID. |
| `noOfEntries` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Maximum number of returned results per page (default: <var>20</var>). |
| `pageNo` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Page number for the required data (default: <var>1</var>). |
| `sortBy` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Sort the result set by specific field. For ascending use <var>+</var>, for descending use <var>-</var>. Allowed values: <var>id</var>, <var>name</var>. (Default: <var>-createdAt</var>). |
| `cmpType` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Type of template to filter. <var>1</var>: Advanced, <var>2</var>: PG. If not provided, all types are returned. |

<details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem" }}>
  <summary style={{ fontSize: "16px" }}>Response properties</summary>

<table className="objectProperties">
<tr>

  <td>Number of records matching the filter.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td>`totalRecords` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
  <td>Total number of templates.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
  <td>`templateList` <br /><span class="type-text">array of objects</span></td>
  <td>List of template objects.</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td colSpan="2">
  <details className="objectPropertiesDetails">
  <summary>`templateList` object properties</summary>
  <table className="objectProperties">
  <tr>
  <td>`id` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
  <td>Template ID.</td>

  <tr>
  <td>`name` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
  <td>Template name.</td>
  </tr>
  <tr>
  <td>`createdAt` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
  <td>Unix epoch timestamp of template creation.</td>

  <tr>
  <td>`type` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
  <td>Template type: <var>1</var> (Advanced), <var>2</var> (PG).</td>

  <td>`creativeType` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>

### Create campaign template

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/template" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Create a new campaign template (Advanced or PG). The template stores campaign configuration settings that can be reused when creating new campaigns.

| Request schema | |
| --- | --- |
| `templateName` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Template name. |
| `templateTypeId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Template type.<br /><var>1</var>: Advanced. <br /><var>2</var>: PG. |

<p />
:::info

The request body accepts the full set of campaign properties for targeting, creative, inventory, and other configurations. See the [Create campaign](#create-new-campaign) and [Create PG campaign](#create-new-pg-campaign) sections for the complete list of supported properties.

:::

<details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem" }}>
  <summary style={{ fontSize: "16px" }}>Response properties</summary>

<table className="objectProperties">
<tr>
  <td>`data` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
  <td>Success message.</td>
</tr>
</table>
</details>

</div><div class="child2">

## Campaign targeting

Configure targeting parameters for campaigns including audiences, creatives, inventory groups, and deals.

### Update audience targeting in campaigns

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/target/audience" operationId="CampaignAudienceTargetingList" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Update the audience targeting in a given campaigns list. Supports include, exclude, and remove audiences.

:::warning

PG campaigns cannot be used for audience targeting.

:::

| Request schema | | 
| --- | --- |
| `{campaignId}` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | [Campaign ID](#get-list-of-campaigns) for which to pass <var>includedAudienceList</var>, <var>excludedAudienceList</var>, and <var>removeAudience</var>. |
| `includedAudienceList` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Audience IDs to include in targeting. |
| `excludedAudienceList` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Audience IDs to exclude from targeting. |
| `removeAudience` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Audience IDs to remove. |

  <details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem" }}>
    <summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>Response properties</summary>
    <table className="objectProperties">
    <tr>
    <td>`message` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>Success message.</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
    <td>`failedCampaigns` <br /><span class="type-text">object</span></td>
    <td>Failed campaigns list: name-value pairs of <var>CampaignId</var> and failure description.</td>
    </tr>
    </table>
    </details>

</div><div class="child2">

```json title="Request schema"
{
  "477717": {
    "includedAudienceList": [
      1064656,
      1060603
    ],
    "excludedAudienceList": [
      1064000,
      1060111
    ],
    "removeAudienceList": [
      106422,
      106033
    ]
  }
}
```

```json title="Response 200"
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "message": "Your changes have been successfully saved."
  }
}
```

```json title="Response 200 (partial success)"
{
  "success": false,
  "data": {
    "message": "Your changes have been successfully saved.",
    "failedCampaigns": {
      "477717": "Can not target more than 20 audiences."
    }
  }
}
```

<details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem" }}>
<summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>More responses</summary>

```json title="Response 422"
{
  "success": false,
  "errorObjects": [
    {
      "error": "One or more campaigns are PG campaigns and cannot be used for audience targeting: [529192, 506992, 506991]"
    }
  ]
}
```
</details>

</div></div>

### Inventory group targeting

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/inventorygroup/{groupId}/includeExclude" operationId="includeExcludeInventoryGroup" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Include or exclude specific campaigns from an inventory Group. Use this to manage which campaigns are targeted by the inventory Group by providing comma-separated lists of campaign IDs to include and/or exclude.

See the Inventory API to [Create a new inventory group](/guidelines/inventory-api#create-a-new-inventory-group).

| Path parameter | |
| --- | --- |
| `groupId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | [Inventory group ID](/guidelines/inventory-api#get-list-of-inventory-groups). |

| Request schema | |
| --- | --- |
| `includedCampaignIds` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Comma-separated [campaign IDs](#get-list-of-campaigns) to include in the inventory group. Use an empty string if no campaigns should be included. |
| `excludedCampaignIds` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Comma-separated [campaign IDs](#get-list-of-campaigns) to exclude from the inventory group. Use an empty string if no campaigns should be excluded. |

  <details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem" }}>
    <summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>Response properties</summary>
    <table className="objectProperties">
    <tr>
    <td>`success` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span></td>
    <td>Indicates whether the operation was successful: <var>true</var>.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`data` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>Success message.</td>
    </tr>
    </table>
    </details>

</div>
<div class="child2">

```json title="Request sample"
{
  "includedCampaignIds": "101,102",
  "excludedCampaignIds": "200"
}
```

```json title="Response 200"
  "success": true,
```

```json title="Response 400"
  "errorObjects": [
```

```json title="Response 401"
      "error": "Invalid token"
    }
  ]
```

### Assign PMP deals

<CopyUrl method="PATCH" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/pmp/campaigns/mappings" operationId="managePMPDealCampaignMappings" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Assign or remove multiple PMP deals to/from campaigns.

| Request schema | |
| --- | --- |
| `operations` <br /><span class="type-text">array of objects</span> | Operations specifying the action, campaign IDs and PMP deal IDs. |
| `action` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Action to perform: <var>assign</var> or <var>deassign</var>. |
| `campaignIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | [Campaign IDs](#get-list-of-campaigns) to assign deals or remove deals from. |
| `pmpDealIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | [PMP deal IDs](/guidelines/inventory-api#get-pmp-deals-list) to assign/de-assign to/from campaigns. |

  <details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem" }}>
    <summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>Response properties</summary>

    <td>`success` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span></td>

    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`data` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>Success message.</td>
    </tr>
    </table>
    </details>
