```json title="Response 403"
{
  "success": false,
  "errorObjects": [
    {
      "error": "Forbidden!"
    }
  ]
}
```

```json title="Response 422"
{
  "success": false,
  "errorObjects": [
    {
      "error": "PMP deal id/s is/are invalid or not accessible for logged-in user"
    }
  ]
}
```

</details>

</div></div>

---

### Assign PG deals

<CopyUrl method="PATCH" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/pg/campaigns/mappings" operationId="managePgDealCampaignMappings" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Assign or remove multiple PG deals to/from campaigns.

| Request schema | |
| --- | --- |
| `operations` <br /><span class="type-text">array of objects</span> | Operations specifying the action, campaign IDs and PMP deal IDs. |
| `action` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Action to perform: <var>assign</var> or <var>deassign</var>. |
| `campaignIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | [Campaign IDs](#get-list-of-campaigns) to assign deals or remove deals from. |
| `pgDealIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | [PG deal ID](/guidelines/inventory-api#get-pg-deals-list)s to assign/remove to/from campaigns. |

  <details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem" }}>
    <summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>Response properties</summary>
    <table className="objectProperties">
        <tr>
    <td>`success` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span></td>
    <td>Indicates Deals were assigned succesfully: <var>true</var>.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`message` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>Success message.</td>
    </tr>
    </table>
    </details>

</div><div class="child2">

```json title="Request sample"
{
  "operations": [
    {
      "action": "assign",
      "campaignIds": [
        201,
        203
      ],
      "pgDealIds": [
        101,
        102
      ]
    },
    {
      "action": "assign",
      "campaignIds": [
        202
      ],
      "pgDealIds": [
        103
      ]
    },
    {
      "action": "deassign",
      "campaignIds": [
        202
      ],
      "pgDealIds": [
        102
      ]
    }
  ]
}
```

```json title="Response 200"
{
  "success": true,
  "data": "PG deal Campaigns mappings updated successfully"
}
```

<details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem" }}>
<summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>More responses</summary>

```json title="Response 403"
{
  "success": false,
  "errorObjects": [
    {
      "error": "Forbidden!"
    }
  ]
}
```

```json title="Response 422"
{
  "success": false,
  "errorObjects": [
    {
      "error": "Campaign ID 506992 exceeds the maximum limit of 25 deals"
    }
  ]
}
```

</details>

</div></div>

## Include/exclude management

The **Include** and **Exclude** options allow you to control where ads appear for a campaign. See the [Allowlist and Blocklist overview<ExternalLink />](https://help.iqm.com/en/articles/10472012-allowlist-and-blocklist-overview) Help Center article for more information.

### Include/exclude device types

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v2/cmp/deviceType/includeExclude" operationId="includeExcludeDeviceType" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Include or exclude device type targeting for a given campaign.

| Request schema | |
| --- | --- |
| `ids` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Comma-separated [device type](/guidelines/master-api#get-device-type) IDs to target or block. |
| `isExcluded` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Target entity: <var>0</var>. <br />Block entity: <var>1</var>. |
| `campaignId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | [Campaign ID](/guidelines/campaign-api#get-list-of-campaigns). |

<details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem"}}>
    <summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>Response properties</summary>
    <table className="objectProperties">
    <tr>
    <td>`message` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>Success message.</td>
    </tr>
    </table>
    </details>

</div>
  <div class="child2">

  <Tabs>
<TabItem value="JSON" label="JSON" default>

```json title="Request sample"
{
  "ids": "15",
  "isExcluded": 0,
  "campaignId": 214269
}
```

```json title="Response 200"
{
  "statusCode": 200,
  "responseObject": {
    "message": "The device types have been allowed."
  }
}
```

</TabItem>
<TabItem value="TypeScript" label="TypeScript">

See [TypeScript prerequisites](/getting-started/typescript-prerequisites.mdx) for usage.

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    content: {
      "application/json;charset=UTF-8": {
        statusCode: number;
        responseObject: {
          message: string;
        };
      };
    };
  };
};

function includeExcludeDeviceType(): Promise<Responses> {
  const options = {
    method: 'POST',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v2/cmp/deviceType/includeExclude',
    requestBody: {
      content: {
        "application/json": {
          ids: `string`,
          isExcluded: `number`,
          campaignId: `number`,
        }
      }
    }
  };

  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

</TabItem></Tabs>

</div></div>

```ts
        "application/json": {
          ids: `string`,
          isExcluded: `number`,
          campaignId: `number`,
        }
      }
    }
  };

  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

</TabItem></Tabs>

</div></div>

---

### Include/exclude campaigns from inventory groups

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/inventorygroup/includeExclude" operationId="includeExcludeInventoryGroup" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Include (whitelist) or exclude (blacklist) campaigns from targeting for one or more [inventory groups](/guidelines/inventory-api#get-list-of-inventory-groups) at once. To target or block campaigns for a single inventory group instead, see [Inventory group targeting](#inventory-group-targeting).

| Request schema | |
| --- | --- |
| `groupIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> <span class="required-text">required</span> | [Inventory group](/guidelines/inventory-api#get-list-of-inventory-groups) IDs to update. |
| `includedCampaignIds` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> <span class="required-text">required</span> | Comma-separated [Campaign ID](/guidelines/campaign-api#get-list-of-campaigns)s to include. |
| `excludedCampaignIds` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> <span class="required-text">required</span> | Comma-separated [Campaign ID](/guidelines/campaign-api#get-list-of-campaigns)s to exclude. |

<details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem"}}>
    <summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>Response properties</summary>
    <table className="objectProperties">
    <tr>
    <td>`data` <br /><span class="type-text">object</span></td>
    <td>Success message.</td>
    </tr>
    </table>
    </details>

</div>
  <div class="child2">

  <Tabs>
<TabItem value="JSON" label="JSON" default>

```json title="Request sample"
{
  "groupIds": [
    12,
    13
  ],
  "includedCampaignIds": "101,102",
  "excludedCampaignIds": "201,202"
}
```

```json title="Response 200"
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "message": "Changes have been saved successfully"
  }
}
```

<details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem" }}>
<summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>More responses</summary>

```json title="Response 400"
{
  "success": false,
  "errorObjects": [
    {
      "error": "Included campaign IDs must be a comma-separated list of numbers."
    }
  ]
}
```

```json title="Response 403"
{
  "success": false,
  "errorObjects": [
    {
      "error": "You are not authorized to perform this operation"
    }
  ]
}
```

```json title="Response 422"
{
  "success": false,
  "errorObjects": [
    {
      "error": "Group ID is required."
    }
  ]
}
```

</details>

</TabItem>
<TabItem value="TypeScript" label="TypeScript">

See [TypeScript prerequisites](/getting-started/typescript-prerequisites.mdx) for usage.

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        success: boolean;
        data: {
          message: string;
        };
      };
    };
  };
};

function includeExcludeInventoryGroups(): Promise<Responses> {
  const options = {
    method: 'POST',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/inventorygroup/includeExclude',
    requestBody: {
      content: {
        "application/json": {
          groupIds: `number[]`,
          includedCampaignIds: `string`,
          excludedCampaignIds: `string`,
        }
      }
    }
  };

  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

</TabItem></Tabs>

</div></div>

## Insertion order details

Insertion orders specify the parameters or details of an advertising campaign.

### Insertion order resource properties

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child3">

| Basic IO resource properties  |  |
|---|---|
| `ioId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Insertion order ID.|
| `ioName` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Insertion order name.|
| `owId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span>  | Organization Workspace ID.|
| `createdByUowId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | User Organization Workspace ID associated with IO creation.|
| `modifiedByUowId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | User Organization Workspace ID associated with IO modification.|
| `ioStartTime` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Unix epoch timestamp (in milliseconds) of IO start time.|
| `ioEndTime` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Unix epoch timestamp (in milliseconds) of IO end time.|
| `ioTotalBudget` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Budget of IO.|
| `ioTimeZoneId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | [Timezone ID](/guidelines/master-api#get-timezones) for insertion order. |
| `isAutoSumIoTotalBudget` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span> | If <var>true</var> Keeps IO budget same as total budget of all included campaigns.|
| `ioBudgetDistributionMethodId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | [Budget distribution method ID](/guidelines/campaign-api#get-list-of-budget-distribution-methods).|
| `ioBudgetTypeId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | [Budget type ID](#get-list-of-campaign-budget-types). |
| `ioTotalImpression` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Number of impressions. |
| `ioStatusID` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | [Status ID](#get-io-status-list). |
| `ioNextPerformanceCheck` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Unix epoch timestamp (in milliseconds) of next performance check.|
| `ioLastPriorityShift` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Unix epoch timestamp (in milliseconds).|
| `ioCurrentPriority` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Current IO campaign priority.|
| `isIoPrioritise` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | All child campaigns will be considered with or without priority.|

</div></div>

### Get insertion order details by ID

<CopyUrl method="GET" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/io/{ioId}" operationId="getBasicIODetailsById" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Get insertion order details by IO ID.

| Path parameter | |
| --- | --- |
| `ioId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | [Insertion order ID](#get-insertion-order-details). |

  <details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem" }}>
    <summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>Response properties</summary>
    <table className="objectProperties">
    <tr>
    <td>`ioId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Insertion order ID.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`ioName` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>Insertion order name.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`ioStartTime` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>IO start time in epoch milliseconds.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`ioEndTime` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>IO end time in epoch milliseconds.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`ioTotalBudget` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Total budget of the insertion order.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`ioTimeZoneId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>[Timezone ID](/guidelines/master-api#get-timezones) for insertion order.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`isBudgetAutoSum` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span></td>
    <td>Whether the IO total budget is auto-summed from campaign budgets.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`ioBudgetDistributionMethodId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>[Budget distribution method ID](#get-list-of-budget-distribution-methods).</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`ioBudgetTypeId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>[Budget type ID](#get-list-of-campaign-budget-types).</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`ioStatusId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>[IO status ID](#get-io-status-list).</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`ioTotalImpressions` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Total impressions for the insertion order.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`owId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Organization Workspace ID.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`createdByUowId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>User Organization Workspace ID of the creator.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`modifiedByUowId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>User Organization Workspace ID of the last modifier.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`isIoPrioritise` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span></td>
    <td>Whether IO prioritization is enabled.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`ioNextPerformanceCheck` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Next performance check time in epoch milliseconds.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>

    <td>Last priority shift time in epoch milliseconds.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>

    <td>Current priority level of the IO.</td>
    </tr>

### Get insertion order details

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/io/basic/list" operationId="getIOBasicDetailsList" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Get a list of insertion order (IO) details.

An insertion order <var>ioId</var> can be used to query insertion order details endpoints or to perform various actions in [Insertion order management](#insertion-order-management).

| Request schema |  |
|---|---|
| `owIdList` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | List of Organization Workspace IDs for which IO details required. |
| `ioIdList` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | List of [insertion order IDs](#get-insertion-order-details) for which IO details required. |
| `ioBudgetTypeIdsList` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | List of [budget type IDs](#get-list-of-campaign-budget-types) for which IO details are required. |
| `ioStatusIdsList` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | List of insertion order [status IDs](#get-io-status-list) for which IO details are required. |
| `ids` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Prioritizes specified IO IDs before others in returned data. |
| `isAllOWIds` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span> | Filters records with all allowed OW IDs if <var>true</var>, prioritizes records over <var>owIds</var>. |
| `pageNo` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Page number of retrieved data.|
| `noOfEntries` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Number of entries returned per page. |
| `searchField` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filter results by search field (supported values: <var>name</var> and <var>id</var>). |
| `sortBy` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Comma separated names of the sorting field. Sorts by ascending (<var>id</var>) or descending (<var>-</var>). <br />Supported values: <var>ioId</var>, <var>ioName</var>, <var>ioStartTime</var>, <var>ioEndTime</var>, <var>ioTotalBudget</var>. |
| `offset` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Offset is alternative of page number. Offset is number of records to be skipped. |

<a href="#insertion-order-resource-properties"><button className="responsePropertiesButton"><Caret />Resource properties</button></a>

</div><div class="child2">

<Tabs>

```json title="Request sample"
}
```

<TabItem value="TypeScript" label="TypeScript">

See [TypeScript prerequisites](/getting-started/typescript-prerequisites.mdx) for usage.

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        success: boolean;
        data: {
          totalRecords: number;
          ioBasicDetailsList: {
            ioId: number;
            ioName: string;
            ioStartTime: number;
            ioEndTime: number;
            ioTimeZoneId: number;
            ioTotalBudget: number;
            ioBudgetDistributionMethodId: number;
            ioBudgetTypeId: number;
            ioStatusId: number;
            ioTotalImpressions: number;
            isBudgetAutoSum: boolean
          }[]
        }
      }
    };
  };
}

function getIOBasicDetailsList(): Promise<Responses> {
  const options = {
    method: 'POST',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/io/basic/list',
    requestBody: {
      content: {
        "application/json": {
          searchBy?: `array of strings`,
          owIdList?: `array of numbers`,
          ids?: `array of numbers`,
          ioIdList?: `array of numbers`,
          ioBudgetTypeIdsList?: `array of numbers`,
          ioStatusIdsList?: `array of numbers`,
          allOWIds?: `boolean`,
          allOwIds?: `boolean`,
          pagination?: `string`,
          pageNo?: `number`,
          noOfEntries?: `number`,
          sortBy?: `string`,
          searchField?: `string`,
          order?: [ "asc" | "desc" ],
          offset?: `number`,
          isAllOwIds?: `boolean`,
        }
      }
    }
  };
  
  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

</TabItem></Tabs>

</div></div>

### Get advanced insertion order details

<CopyUrl method="GET" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/io/advanced/list" operationId="getIOListWithReportData" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Get an advanced list of insertion order details.

| Query parameters |  |
|---|---|
| `pageNo` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Page number of retrieved data. |
| `pageSize` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Number of entries returned per page. |
| `searchField` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filter results by search field. <br />Supported values: <var>name</var> and <var>id</var>. |
| `sortBy` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Sorts by ascending (<var>+</var>) or descending (<var>-</var>), default: <var>-ioId</var>. |
| `offset` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Offset is alternative of page number. Offset is number of records to be skipped. |
| `startDate` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Unix epoch timestamp of start date for returned data, in milliseconds. |
| `endDate` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Unix epoch timestamp of end date for returned data, in milliseconds. |
| `budgetTypeIds` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Comma separated [budget type IDs](#get-list-of-campaign-budget-types) to include in data. |
| `ioStatusIds` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Comma separated [status IDs](#get-io-status-list) to include in data. |

<a href="#insertion-order-resource-properties"><button className="responsePropertiesButton"><Caret />Resource properties</button></a>

```ts
function getIOListWithReportData(): Promise<Responses> {
  const options = {
    method: 'GET',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/io/advanced/list',
    parameters: {
      query?: {
        searchField?: `string`,
        pageSize?: `number`,
        offset?: `number`,
        pageNo?: `number`,
        sortBy?: `string`,
        startDate?: `number`,
        endDate?: `number`,
        budgetTypeIds?: `string`,
        timeZoneId?: `number`,
        ioStatusIds?: `string`,
      }
    }
  };
  
  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

### Download advanced IO details

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/io/advanced/list/download" operationId="downloadIOReport" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Download advanced IO details as CSV or XLSX file. 

| Request schema |  |
|---|---|
| `pageNo` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Page number of retrieved data. |
| `noOfEntries` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Number of entries returned per page. |
| `searchField` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filter results by search field. <br />Supported values: <var>name</var> and <var>id</var>. |
| `sortBy` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Sorts by ascending (<var>+</var>) or descending (<var>-</var>), default: <var>-ioId</var>. |
| `ioIdsList` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | IO IDs to include in data.
| `timezoneId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | [Timezone ID](/guidelines/master-api#get-timezones).
| `ioStatusIdsList` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | [Status IDs](#get-io-status-list) to include in data. |
| `budgetTypeIdList` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | [Budget type IDs](#get-list-of-campaign-budget-types) to include in data. |
| `columns` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Column names to display in report as specified by <var>label</var> and <var>value</var> properties.
| `startDate` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Unix epoch timestamp of start date for returned data, in milliseconds. |
| `endDate` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Unix epoch timestamp of end date for returned data, in milliseconds. | 
| `fileType` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | File type to download: <var>csv</var> or <var>xlsx</var>.
| `fileName` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | File name.
| `owIds` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Download file will only contain data for comma separated Customer OW IDs.

  <details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem" }}>
    <summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>Response properties</summary>
    <table className="objectProperties">
        <tr>
    <td>`url` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>File download URL.</td>
    </tr>
    </table>
    </details>


</div>
<div class="child2">

### Get IO campaign budget and details

<CopyUrl method="GET" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/io/campaign/budget-impression/info" operationId="getBudgetAndImpressionInfoByIOId" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Get an IO's campaigns budget and spending details.

| Query parameters ||
| --- | --- |
| `ioId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | [Insertion order ID](#get-insertion-order-details) to retrieve details for. |

 <details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem" }}>
    <summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>Response properties</summary>
    <table className="objectProperties">
        <tr>
    <td>`totalAllocatedBudget` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Total budget allocated to IO's campaigns.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`totalSpentBudget` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Total budget spent by IO's campaigns.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`totalAllocatedImpression` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Total impressions allocated to IO's campaigns.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`totalSpentImpression` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Total impressions spent by IO's campaigns.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`totalImpressions` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Total impressions count of IO's campaigns.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`totalBudget` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Total budget of IO's campaigns.</td>
    </tr>
    </table>
    </details>

</div><div class="child2">

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        success: boolean;
        data: {
          totalAllocatedBudget: number;
          totalSpentBudget: number;
          totalAllocatedImpression: number;
          totalSpentImpression: number;
          totalImpressions: number;
          totalBudget: number;
        }
      }
    };
  };
};

function getBudgetAndImpressionInfoByIOId(): Promise<Responses> {
  const options = {
    method: 'GET',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/io/campaign/budget-impression/info',
    parameters: {
      query: {
        ioId: `number`
      }
    }
  };
  
  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

### Get list of campaign details grouped by insertion order ID

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/io/campaign/basic/list" operationId="getIoMappedWithCampaignsDetails" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Get a list of campaigns details grouped by insertion order ID with supported filters.

| Request schema |  |
| ----  | --- |
| `pageNo` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Page number of retrieved data, default: <var>1</var>. |
| `noOfEntries` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Number of entries returned per page, default: <var>20</var>. |
| `sortBy` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Sorts by ascending (<var>+</var>) or descending (<var>-</var>) Supported values: <var>ioId</var>, <var>ioName</var>, <var>ioStartTime</var>, <var>ioEndTime</var>, <var>ioTotalBudget</var>, <var>campaignId</var>, <var>isRecordTargeted</var>. |
| `searchField` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filter results by search field. |
| `ioIdsList` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filter by [insertion order IDs](#get-insertion-order-details). Records matching provided IDs will be returned first, before others. |
| `campaignTypeIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filter results by [campaign type IDs](#get-campaign-count-with-campaign-type). |
| `ids` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filters for [campaign IDs](#get-list-of-campaigns). |
| `ioIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filters for [insertion order IDs](#get-insertion-order-details). |
| `status` <br /><span class="type-text">array of strings</span> | Filters for types of campaign Status. |
| `creativeTypeIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filters for [creative type IDs](/guidelines/creative-api/#get-creative-types-list).  |
| `ioBudgetTypeIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filters for [budget type IDs](#get-list-of-campaign-budget-types). |
| `owIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filters for Customer Organization Workspace IDs. |
| `offset` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Offset is alternative of page number. Offset is number of records to be skipped. |


<a href="#insertion-order-resource-properties"><button className="responsePropertiesButton"><Caret />Resource properties (IO)</button></a>

<p /><a href="#campaign-resource-properties"><button className="responsePropertiesButton"><Caret />Resource properties (Campaign)</button></a>

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        success: boolean;
        data: {
          totalRecords: number;
          filteredRecords: number;
          ioCampaignsList: {
            ioId: number;
            ioname: string;
            ioBudgetTypeId: number;
            created: number;
            lastModified: number;
            owId: number;
            uowId: number;
            campaignCount: number;
            campaigns: {
              id: number;
              campaignId: number;
              name: string;
              creativeTypeId: number;
              campaignTypeId: number;
              status: string;
              startTime: number;
              endTime: number;
              createdAt: number;
              modifiedAt: number;
              owId: number;
              timezoneId: number;
              targetedAudienceIds: number[];
            }[]
          }[]
        }
      }
    }
  };
};

function getIoMappedWithCampaignsDetails(): Promise<Responses> {
  const options = {
    method: 'POST',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/io/campaign/basic/list',
    requestBody: {
      content: {
        "application/json": {
          searchBy?: `array of strings`,
          status?: `array of strings`,
          creativeTypeIds?: `array of numbers`,
          owIds?: `array of numbers`,
          ioIds?: `array of numbers`,
          ids?: `array of numbers`,
          campaignIds?: `array of numbers`,
          campaignTypeIds?: `array of numbers`,
          ioBudgetTypeIds?: `array of numbers`,
          paymentTypeId?: `number`,
          includeTargetedAudienceList?: `boolean`,
          campaignExpiredAfterEpoch?: `number`,
          allOwIds?: `boolean`,
          pagination?: `string`,
          pageNo?: `number`,
          noOfEntries?: `number`,
          sortBy?: `string`,
          searchField?: `string`,
          order?: [ "asc" | "desc" ],
          offset?: `number`,
        }
      }
    }
  };
  
  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

### Get list of campaign details grouped by IO ID with filters

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/io/campaign/served/list" operationId="getServedIoMappedWithCampaignsDetails" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Get a list of insertion order details along with campaigns details attached to that IO based on filters.

| Request schema |  |
| ----  | --- |
| `pageNo` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Page number of retrieved data, default: <var>1</var>. |
| `noOfEntries` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Number of entries returned per page, default: <var>20</var>. |
| `sortBy` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Sorts by ascending (<var>+</var>) or descending (<var>-</var>) Supported values: <var>ioId</var>, <var>ioName</var>, <var>ioStartTime</var>, <var>ioEndTime</var>, <var>ioTotalBudget</var>, <var>campaignId</var>, <var>isRecordTargeted</var>. |
| `searchField` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filter results by search field. |
| `ioIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filter by [insertion order IDs](#get-insertion-order-details). Records matching provided IDs will be returned first, before others. |
| `campaignIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filters results by provided [campaign IDs](#get-list-of-campaigns). |
| `status` <br /><span class="type-text">array of strings</span> | Filters for types of campaign Status. |
| `creativeTypeIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filters for [creative type IDs](/guidelines/creative-api/#get-creative-types-list).  |
| `owIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filters for Customer Organization Workspace IDs. |
| `isAllOwIds` <br /><span class="type-text">object</span> | Filters records with all allowed OW IDs if <var>true</var>, prioritizes records over <var>owIds</var>. |
| `startDate` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> <span class="required-text">required</span> | Returns IOs with start date greater than date provided (should not be older than a year). |
| `endDate` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> <span class="required-text">required</span> | Returns IOs with start date less than date provided (should not be older than a year).|

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    headers: {
      [name: string]: unknown;
    };
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        success: boolean;
        data: {
          ioCampaignsList: {
            ioId: number;
            ioName: string;
            ioBudgetTypeId: number;
            ioStatusId: number;
            created: number;
            lastModified: number;
            owId: number;
            uowId: number;
            campaignCount: number;
            campaigns: {
              creativeTypeId: number;
              id: number;
              name: string;
              owId: number;
              status: string;
              lastModified: number;
              uowId: number;
              organizationName: string;
              organizationLogo: string;
            }[]
          }
        }
        totalRecords: number;
        filteredRecords: number;
      }
    };
  };
};

function getServedIoMappedWithCampaignsDetails(): Promise<Responses> {
  const options = {
    method: 'POST',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/io/campaign/served/list',
    requestBody: {
      content: {
        "application/json": {
          searchBy?: `array of strings`,
          status?: `array of strings`,
          creativeTypeIds?: `array of numbers`,
          owIds?: `array of numbers`,
          ioIds?: `array of numbers`,
          ids?: `array of numbers`,
          campaignIds?: `array of numbers`,
          campaignTypeIds?: `array of numbers`,
          ioBudgetTypeIds?: `array of numbers`,
          paymentTypeId?: `number`,
          includeTargetedAudienceList?: `boolean`,
          campaignExpiredAfterEpoch?: `number`,
          startDate?: `number`,
          endDate?: `number`,
          allOwIds?: `boolean`,
          pagination?: `string`,
          pageNo?: `number`,
          noOfEntries?: `number`,
          sortBy?: `string`,
          searchField?: `string`,
          order?: [ "asc" | "desc" ],
          offset?: `number`,
        }
      }
    }
  };
  
  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

### Get list of campaigns and report details by insertion order ID

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/io/campaigns/list" operationId="getCampaignIODetailsByIoId" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Get a list of campaigns with detailed report by insertion order ID (<var>ioId</var>) with supported filters.

| Request schema |  |
| ----  | --- |
| `pageNo` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Page number of retrieved data, default: <var>1</var>. |
| `noOfEntries` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Number of entries returned per page, default: <var>20</var>. |
| `sortBy` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Sorts by ascending (<var>+</var>) or descending (<var>-</var>) (See supported values in collapsible section below table.). |
| `searchField` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filter results by search field. |
| `ioIdsList` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filter by [insertion order IDs](#get-insertion-order-details). Records matching provided IDs will be returned first, before others. |
| `budgetTypeIdList` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filters for selected [budget type IDs](#get-list-of-campaign-budget-types) e.g. "impression based" or "budget based". Records matching the provided IDs will be returned. |
| `campaignIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filters for selected [campaign IDs](#get-list-of-campaigns). Records matching the provided IDs will be returned first, before others. |
| `timeZoneId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Filters campaigns for specified [timezone ID](/guidelines/master-api#get-timezones). |
| `campaignStatusList` <br /><span class="type-text">array of strings</span> | Filters campaigns by specified campaign Status. |
| `creativeTypeIds` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filters campaigns by specified [creative type ID](/guidelines/creative-api/#get-creative-types-list).  |
| `owIds` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filters campaigns by specified Customer Organization Workspace ID. |

### Download CSV/XLSX file for IO-based campaign details

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/io/campaigns/list/download" operationId="downloadCampaignsReport" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Create a downloadable report of campaign details based on insertion order.

| Request schema | |
| --- | --- |
| `searchField` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filters results by search field. |
| `noOfEntries` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Number of entries returned per page, default: <var>20</var>. |
| `pageNo` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Page number of retrieved data, default: <var>1</var>. |
| `sortBy` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Sorts by ascending (<var>+</var>) or descending (<var>-</var>). |
| `owIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filter results by Organization Workspace IDs. |
| `ioIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Report will include provided campaign IO IDs. |
| `campaignIds` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Comma separated [campaign IDs](#get-list-of-campaigns) to include in report. |
| `timeZoneId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Filters data in report for provided [timezone ID](/guidelines/master-api#get-timezones). |
| `campaignStatus` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Comma separated campaign statuses to include in report. |
| `creativeTypeIds` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filters report for provided creative type IDs only. |
| `columns` <br /><span class="type-text">object</span> <span class="required-text">required</span> | Column names to display in report as specified by <var>label</var> and <var>value</var> properties. |
| `startDate` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> <span class="required-text">required</span> | Unix epoch start date for data in report, in milliseconds. |
| `endDate`  <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> <span class="required-text">required</span> | Unix epoch end date for data in report, in milliseconds. |
| `fileType` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> <span class="required-text">required</span> | Specifies file type for download: <var>csv</var> or <var>xlsx</var>. |
| `fileName` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> <span class="required-text">required</span> | Specifies file name for download. |

  <details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem" }}>
    <summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>Response properties</summary>
    <table className="objectProperties">
        <tr>
    <td>`url` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>File download URL.</td>
    </tr>
    </table>
    </details>

</div><div class="child2">

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        success: boolean;
        data: {
          url: string
        }
      }
    };
  };
  422: {
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        success: boolean;
        errorObjects: {
          error: string;
        }[]
      }
    };
  };
};

function downloadCampaignsReport(): Promise<Responses> {
  const options = {
    method: 'POST',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/io/campaigns/list/download',
    requestBody: {
      content: {
        "application/json": {
          startDate?: `number`,
          endDate?: `number`,
          ioIdsList?: `array of numbers`,
          timezoneId?: `number`,
          campaignStatus?: `string`,
          creativeTypeIds?: `string`,
          owIds?: `string`,
          conversionIds?: `string`,
          fileType?: `string`,
          fileName?: `string`,
          campaignIds?: `string`,
          owIdsList?: `array of numbers`,
          campaignTypeIds?: `array of numbers`,
          pagination?: `string`,
          pageNo?: `number`,
          noOfEntries?: `number`,
          sortBy?: `string`,
          searchField?: `string`,
          order?: [ "asc" | "desc" ],
          offset?: `number`,
        }
      }
    }
  };
  
  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

### Get list of IO start/end dates

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/io/startDate/list" operationId="getIOStartDateList" />
<br /><CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/io/endDate/list" operationId="getIOEndDateList" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

| Request schema | |
| --- | --- |
| `noOfEntries` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Number of entries returned per page, default: <var>300</var>.|
| `pageNo` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Page number of retrieved data, default: <var>1</var>. |
| `sortBy` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Sorts by ascending (<var>+</var>) or descending (<var>-</var>), default: <var>-ioDate</var>. |
| `owIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Filter results by Organization Workspace IDs. |
| `isAllOwIds` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span> | Filters records with all allowed OW IDs if <var>true</var>, prioritizes records over <var>owIds</var>. |
| `ids` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | Prioritizes results of provided IDs over other records. |
| `startDate` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filters for IOs with start date greater than the one specified. |
| `endDate`  <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Filters for IOs with a date less than the one specified. |

  <details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem" }}>
    <summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>Response properties</summary>
    <table className="objectProperties">
        <tr>
    <td>`ioStartDateList` <br /><span class="type-text">array of strings</span></td>
    <td>List of IO start dates.</td>
    </tr>
    </table>
    </details>

</div><div class="child2">

<Tabs>
<TabItem value="JSON" label="JSON" default>

```json title="Request sample"
}
```

```ts
import {

/*

/*

          sortBy?: `string`,
          searchField?: `string`,
          order?: [ "asc" | "desc" ],
        }
      }
    }
  };
  
  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    headers: {
      [name: string]: unknown;
    };
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        success: boolean;
        data: {
          ioId: number;
          ioName: string;
          ioStartTime: number;
          ioEndTime: number;
          ioTotalBudget: number;
          ioTimeZoneId: number;
          ioBudgetTypeId: number;
          ioBudgetDistributionMethodId: number;
          isAutoSumIoTotalBudget: boolean;
          ioTotalImpressions: number
        }
      }
    };
  };
};

function createCampaignIO(): Promise<Responses> {
  const options = {
    method: 'POST',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/io/add',
    requestBody: {
      content: {
        "application/json": {
          ioId?: `number`,
          ioName: `string`,
          owId?: `number`,
          createdByUowId?: `number`,
          modifiedByUowId?: `number`,
          ioStartTime?: `number`,
          ioEndTime?: `number`,
          ioTotalBudget?: `number`,
          ioTimeZoneId?: `number`,
          isAutoSumIoTotalBudget?: `boolean`,
          ioBudgetDistributionMethodId?: `number`,
          ioBudgetTypeId?: `number`,
          ioTotalImpressions?: `number`,
          ioStatusId?: `number`,
          ioNextPerformanceCheck?: `number`,
          ioLastPriorityShift?: `number`,
          ioCurrentPriority?: `number`,
          isIoPrioritise?: `boolean`,
        }
      }
    }
  };
  
  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

### Update insertion order details

<CopyUrl method="PATCH" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/io/{ioId}" operationId="editIODetails" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Update various properties of a given insertion order. 

| Path parameters |  |
| ----  | --- |
| `ioId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | [Insertion order ID](#get-insertion-order-details). |

| Request schema | |
| --- | --- |
| `ioName` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span> | Update insertion order name. |
| `ioStartTime`<br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> |  Unix epoch timestamp (in milliseconds) of IO start time. |
| `ioEndTime` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> |  Unix epoch timestamp (in milliseconds) of IO end time. |
| `ioTotalBudget` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Budget of IO. |
| `ioTimeZoneId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | [Timezone ID](/guidelines/master-api#get-timezones). |
| `ioTotalImpressions` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Number of impressions. |
| `isAutoSumIoTotalBudget` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span> | If <var>true</var> Keeps IO budget same as total budget of all included campaigns. |

  <details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem" }}>
    <summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>Response properties</summary>
    <table className="objectProperties">
       <tr>
    <td>`success` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span></td>
    <td>Indicates IO was succesfully updated: <var>true</var>.</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
    <td>`data` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>Success message.</td>
    </tr>
    </table>
    </details>
 
</div><div class="child2">

<div class="endpoint-container">

### Duplicate an insertion order

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/io/duplicate" operationId="duplicateIO" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Duplicate a single IO, and all campaigns within that IO.

| Request schema |  |
| ---- | --- |
| `ioExistingCampaignIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | [Campaign IDs](#get-list-of-campaigns) within specified IO (<var>ioId</var>). |
| `ioId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Specified IO to duplicate. |
| `ioTimeZoneId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | [Timezone ID](/guidelines/master-api#get-timezones). |
| `ioEndTime` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Unix epoch of IO end time. |
| `ioStartTime` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Unix epoch of IO start time. |

  <details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem" }}>
    <summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>Response properties</summary>
    <table className="objectProperties">
       <tr>
    <td>`duplicatedCampaignIds` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span></td>
    <td>Duplicated campaign IDs.</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
    <td>`duplicatedIOId` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Duplicated IO ID.</td>
    </tr>
    </table>
    </details>

</div><div class="child2">

<Tabs>
<TabItem value="JSON" label="JSON" default>

```json title="Request sample"
{
    "ioStartTime": 1698295604000,
    "ioEndTime": 1698258600000,
    "ioId": 223,
    "ioTimeZoneId": 105,
    "ioExistingCampaignIds": [
        12345,
        67890,
        21345
    ]
}
```

```json title="Response 200"
{
    "success": true,
    "data": {
        "duplicatedCampaignIds": [
          12345,
          54321
        ],
        "duplicatedIOId": 271
    }
}
```

</TabItem>
<TabItem value="TypeScript" label="TypeScript">

See [TypeScript prerequisites](/getting-started/typescript-prerequisites.mdx) for usage. 

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    headers: {
      [name: string]: unknown;
    };
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        success: boolean;
        data: {
          duplicatedCampaignIds: number[];
          duplicatedIOId: number
        }
      }
    };
  };
};

function duplicateIO(): Promise<Responses> {
  const options = {
    method: 'PATCH',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/io/duplicate',
    requestBody: {
      content: {
        "application/json": {
          ioExistingCampaignIds?: `array of numbers`,
          ioId?: `number`,
          ioTimeZoneId?: `number`,
          ioEndTime?: `number`,
          ioStartTime?: `number`,
        }
      }
    }
  };
  
  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

</TabItem></Tabs>

</div></div>

### Delete an insertion order

<CopyUrl method="POST" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/io/delete" operationId="updateIOStatusToDelete" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Delete one or multiple IOs and all campaigns attached to them.


| Request schema |  |
| ---- | --- |
| `ioIdsList` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span> | [Insertion order IDs](#get-insertion-order-details). |

  <details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem" }}>
    <summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>Response properties</summary>
    <table className="objectProperties">
       <tr>
    <td>`success` <br /><span class="type-text">boolean</span></td>
    <td>Indicates IO was succesfully updated: <var>true</var>.</td>
    </tr>
        <tr>
    <td>`data` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>Success message.</td>
    </tr>
    </table>
    </details>

</div><div class="child2">

<Tabs>
<TabItem value="JSON" label="JSON" default>

```json title="Request sample"
{
    "ioIdsList": [
        1,
        2,
        3
    ]
}
```

```json title="Response 200"
{
    "success": true,
    "data": "3 Insertion Orders deleted successfully"
}
```

</TabItem>
<TabItem value="TypeScript" label="TypeScript">

See [TypeScript prerequisites](/getting-started/typescript-prerequisites.mdx) for usage.

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    headers: {
      [name: string]: unknown;
    };
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        success: boolean;
        data: string
      }
    };
  };
};

function updateIOStatusToDelete(): Promise<Responses> {
  const options = {
    method: 'POST',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/io/delete',
    requestBody: {
      content: {
        "application/json": {
          ioIdsList: `array of numbers`,
          owIdsList?: `array of numbers`,
        }
      }
    }
  };
  
  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

</TabItem></Tabs>

</div></div>

## Get more details

### Get frequency cap types

<CopyUrl method="GET" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/static/frequency-cap-types" operationId="getFrequencyCapTypeList" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Get list of frequency cap types for campaign configuration.

| Query parameter | |
| --- | --- |
| `frequencyCapTypeIds` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span> | Filter by specific frequency cap type IDs (optional). |

  <details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem" }}>
    <summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>Response properties</summary>
    <table className="objectProperties">
    <tr>
    <td>`frequencyCapTypes` <br /><span class="type-text">array of objects</span></td>
    <td>List of frequency cap types.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`id` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Frequency cap type ID.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`name` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>Frequency cap type name: <var>days</var>, <var>weeks</var>, <var>months</var>, <var>campaign_duration</var>.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`displayName` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>Display name.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`minDuration` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Minimum duration value.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`maxDuration` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Maximum duration value.</td>
    </tr>
    </table>
    </details>

</div><div class="child2">

```json title="Response 200"
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "frequencyCapTypes": [
      {
        "id": 1,
        "name": "days",
        "order": 1,
        "displayName": "Day(s)",
        "minDuration": 1,
        "maxDuration": 6
      },
      {
        "id": 2,
        "name": "weeks",
        "order": 2,
        "displayName": "Week(s)",
        "minDuration": 1,
        "maxDuration": 3
      },
      {
        "id": 3,
        "name": "months",
        "order": 3,
        "displayName": "Month(s)",
        "minDuration": 1,
        "maxDuration": 3
      },
      {
        "id": 4,
        "name": "campaign_duration",
        "order": 4,
        "displayName": "Campaign Duration",
        "minDuration": null,
        "maxDuration": null
      }
    ],
    "totalRecords": 4,
    "filteredRecords": 4
  }
}
```

</div></div>

### AI-Based Optimization Campaign Goals and Objectives

<CopyUrl method="GET" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/static/campaign/goal-ai/objectives-goals" operationId="getCampaignGoalAIObjectiveAndGoals" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Get static list of AI-Based Optimization Campaign Goals and Campaign Objectives.

See the [Optimization & delivery overview - AI-Based Optimization (Automated)<ExternalLink />](https://help.iqm.com/en/articles/10906959-optimization-delivery-overview#h_4753688f6d) Help Center article for more information on Goal-AI objectives and goals.

  <details className="objectPropertiesDetails" style={{ maxWidth: "100%", padding: "1rem" }}>
    <summary style={{fontSize: "16px"}}>Response properties</summary>
    <table className="objectProperties">
    <tr>
    <td>`objectives` <br /><span class="type-text">array of objects</span></td>
    <td>List of Campaign Objectives: <var>awareness</var>, <var>consideration</var>, <var>conversion</var>.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`goals` <br /><span class="type-text">array of objects</span></td>
    <td>List of Campaign Goals with their compatible creative types and KPI ranges.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`id` <br /><span class="type-text">integer</span></td>
    <td>Campaign Objective or Campaign Goal ID.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`name` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>Internal name.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`displayName` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>Display name.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`applicableGoals` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span></td>
    <td>Campaign Goal IDs that apply to the Campaign Objective.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`compatibleCreativeTypes` <br /><span class="type-text">array of integers</span></td>
    <td>[Creative type IDs](/guidelines/creative-api#get-creative-types-list) compatible with the Campaign Goal.</td>
    </tr>
    <tr>
    <td>`kpi` <br /><span class="type-text">string</span></td>
    <td>KPI metric name.</td>
    </tr>
    </table>
    </details>

</div><div class="child2">

```json title="Response 200"
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "objectives": [
      {
        "id": 1,
        "name": "awareness",
        "displayName": "Awareness",
        "applicableGoals": [1, 2, 3, 4, 5, 8],
        "displayOrder": 1
      },
      {
        "id": 2,
        "name": "consideration",
        "displayName": "Consideration",
        "applicableGoals": [9, 10, 2, 3, 4],
        "displayOrder": 2
      },
      {
        "id": 3,
        "name": "conversion",
        "displayName": "Conversion",
        "applicableGoals": [11, 6, 7],
        "displayOrder": 3
      }
    ],
    "goals": [
      {
        "id": 1,
        "name": "increase_reach",
        "displayName": "Increase Reach",
        "compatibleCreativeTypes": [11, 13, 14, 15, 17],
        "kpi": null,
        "minKpi": 1,
        "maxKpi": 100,
        "displayOrder": 1
      },
      {
        "id": 2,
        "name": "increase_ctr",
        "displayName": "Increase CTR",
        "compatibleCreativeTypes": [11, 13, 14, 15, 17],
        "kpi": "CTR",
        "minKpi": 1,
        "maxKpi": 100,
        "displayOrder": 2
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

<TabItem value="TypeScript" label="TypeScript">

See [TypeScript prerequisites](/getting-started/typescript-prerequisites.mdx) for usage.

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    headers: {
      [name: string]: unknown;
    };
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        success: boolean;
        data: {
          ioStatusList: {
            name: string;
            id: number;
            label: string;
            order: number
          }[];
          totalRecords: number;
          filteredRecords: number
        }
      }
    };
  };
};

function getIOStatusTypeList(): Promise<Responses> {
  const options = {
    method: 'GET',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/io/status/list',
  };
  
  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

### Get list of campaign budget types

<CopyUrl method="GET" url="https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/static/campaign/budget-type" operationId="getBudgetTypeList" />

<div class="endpoint-container">
  <div class="child1">

Get a list of budget types for campaigns and insertion orders. 

See the [Budget-based and impression-based insertion order (IO) overview<ExternalLink />](https://help.iqm.com/en/articles/8481053-budget-based-and-impression-based-insertion-order-io-overview) Help Center article for more information.

| Properties | |
| --- | --- |
| `1` <br /><span class="type-text">Budget</span> | Campaigns serve with set budget amount. |
| `2` <br /><span class="type-text">Impression</span> | Campaigns serve with set target for impressions to generate. |

</div><div class="child2">

<Tabs>
<TabItem value="JSON" label="JSON" default>

```json title="Response 200"
{
  "success": true,
  "data": {
    "totalRecords": 2,
    "filteredRecords": 2,
    "budgetTypeData": [
      {
        "id": 1,
        "name": "budget",
        "label": "Budget",
        "order": 1
      },
      {
        "id": 2,
        "name": "impression",
        "label": "Impression",
        "order": 2
      }
    ]
  }
}
```

</TabItem>
<TabItem value="TypeScript" label="TypeScript">

See [TypeScript prerequisites](/getting-started/typescript-prerequisites.mdx) for usage. 

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    headers: {
      [name: string]: unknown;
    };
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        success: boolean;
        data: {
          totalRecords: number;
          filteredRecords: number;
          budgetTypeData: {
            id: number;
            name: string;
            label: string;
            order: number;
          }[]
        }
      }
    };
  };
};

function getBudgetTypeList(): Promise<Responses> {
  const options = {
    method: 'GET',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/static/campaign/budget-type',
  };
  
  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```

</TabItem></Tabs>

</div></div>

<div class="endpoint-container">

```ts
import {
  getInstance
} from "prerequisites"

const axios = getInstance();

interface Responses {
  200: {
    content: {
      "application/json": {
        success: boolean;
        data: {
          budgetDistributionMethodData: {
            name: string;
            id: number;
            label: string;
            order: number;
          }[];
          totalRecords: number;
          filteredRecords: number;
        }
      }
    };
  };
};

function getStaticBudgetDistributionList(): Promise<Responses> {
  const options = {
    method: 'GET',
    url: 'https://api.iqm.com/api/v3/cmp/static/budget-distribution-method/list',
    parameters: {
      query?: {
        budgetDistributionMethod?: `number`,
      }
    }
  };
  
  return axios.request(options).then(({ data }: { data: Responses }) => data);
}
```
